1	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	import { Chip, Switch, TextField } from '@/design/components';
3	import type { DraftGuest } from '@/lib/types';
4	
5	type GuestEdit = {
6	  include?: boolean;
7	  name?: string;
8	  phone?: string;
9	};
10	
11	export function RosterRow({
12	  guest,
13	  onChange,
14	}: {
15	  guest: DraftGuest;
16	  onChange: (edit: GuestEdit) => void;
17	}) {
18	  const [name, setName] = useState(guest.name);
19	  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(guest.phone ?? '');
20	
21	  // Keep local inputs in sync when the server reconciles the row.
22	  useEffect(() => setName(guest.name), [guest.name]);
23	  useEffect(() => setPhone(guest.phone ?? ''), [guest.phone]);
24	
25	  const sent = guest.status === 'sent';
26	  const cancelled = guest.status === 'cancelled';
27	
28	  return (
29	    <div className={`row${guest.include ? '' : ' row--dim'}`}>
30	      <Switch
31	        checked={guest.include}
32	        disabled={sent}
33	        onChange={(v) => onChange({ include: v })}
34	        label={`Invite ${guest.email}`}
35	      />
36	
37	      <div className="row__grow">
38	        <div style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 8 }}>
39	          <span className="type-label row__ellipsis" style={{ flex: '0 1 auto' }}>
40	            {guest.name || guest.email}
41	          </span>
42	          {guest.internal && !sent && <Chip>Internal</Chip>}
43	          {sent && <Chip tone="success">Pass sent</Chip>}
44	          {cancelled && <Chip tone="error">Cancelled</Chip>}
45	        </div>
46	        <div className="type-label-sm text-muted row__ellipsis">{guest.email}</div>
47	
48	        {guest.include && !sent && (
49	          <div style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 8, marginTop: 10 }}>
50	            <TextField
51	              label="Visitor name"
52	              value={name}
53	              onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}
54	              onBlur={() => name !== guest.name && onChange({ name })}
55	              placeholder="Full name"
56	            />
57	            <TextField
58	              label="Phone (for WhatsApp pass)"
59	              value={phone}
60	              inputMode="tel"
61	              onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
62	              onBlur={() => phone !== (guest.phone ?? '') && onChange({ phone })}
63	              placeholder="+91…"
64	            />
65	          </div>
66	        )}
67	      </div>
68	    </div>
69	  );
70	}
71

1	import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
2	import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
3	import {
4	  AlertCircle,
5	  ArrowLeft,
6	  CheckCircle2,
7	  Clock,
8	  DoorOpen,
9	  Eye,
10	  RefreshCw,
11	} from 'lucide-react';
12	import {
13	  Button,
14	  Card,
15	  IconButton,
16	  Logo,
17	  SelectField,
18	  Skeleton,
19	  TextField,
20	} from '@/design/components';
21	import {
22	  ACTIVE_EID_KEY,
23	  ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY,
24	  REFRESH_ACTIVE,
25	  RpcError,
26	  rpc,
27	} from '@/lib/messaging';
28	import type { PreviewResponse } from '@/lib/types';
29	import {
30	  useActiveEid,
31	  useActiveSnapshot,
32	  useAuthStatus,
33	  useDraft,
34	  usePatchDraft,
35	  usePreview,
36	  useResolveEvent,
37	  useSend,
38	  useVisitorEvents,
39	} from './hooks';
40	import { AccountMenu } from './AccountMenu';
41	import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
42	import { RosterRow } from './RosterRow';
43	import { PreviewSheet } from './PreviewSheet';
44	import { SignInGate } from './SignInGate';
45	
46	const isAuthError = (err: unknown) => err instanceof RpcError && !!err.needsAuth;
47	
48	export function VisitPanel() {
49	  // `storedEid` is what the content script is pointing us at (auto-follow). We
50	  // freeze the *displayed* `eid` while busy so following never switches the view
51	  // mid-action (Guard 1); edits already persist on blur (Guard 2).
52	  const storedEid = useActiveEid();
53	  const snapshot = useActiveSnapshot();
54	  const [eid, setEid] = useState<string | null>(storedEid);
55	  const auth = useAuthStatus();
56	  // Picker fuels the "open from list" empty state (and graceful fallback).
57	  const visitorEvents = useVisitorEvents(!eid && !!auth.data?.signedIn);
58	  const resolve = useResolveEvent(eid);
59	  const event = resolve.data;
60	  const draft = useDraft(event);
61	
62	  const patch = usePatchDraft(event?.iCalUid);
63	  const send = useSend(event?.iCalUid, event?.start, event?.end);
64	  const preview = usePreview(event?.iCalUid);
65	
66	  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<{
67	    preview: PreviewResponse;
68	    recipient: string;
69	  } | null>(null);
70	  const [location, setLocation] = useState<string | null>(null);
71	
72	  // Guard 1: hold the current event while sending / previewing; catch up once idle.
73	  const busy = send.isPending || preview.isPending || !!previewData;
74	  useEffect(() => {
75	    if (!busy) setEid(storedEid);
76	  }, [storedEid, busy]);
77	
78	  // Post-save refresh: the background sync poll broadcasts when the active event
79	  // changed on the server (no DOM); refetch so the roster reflects saved state.
80	  const qc = useQueryClient();
81	  useEffect(() => {
82	    const listener = (msg: { type?: string }) => {
83	      if (msg?.type === REFRESH_ACTIVE) {
84	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['resolve'] });
85	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['draft'] });
86	      }
87	    };
88	    chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener(listener);
89	    return () => chrome.runtime.onMessage.removeListener(listener);
90	  }, [qc]);
91	
92	  // Back → clear the active event/snapshot, returning to the home list.
93	  const clearActive = () =>
94	    chrome.storage.session.remove([ACTIVE_EID_KEY, ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]);
95	  // Show Back in every screen except the pure home (no event, no snapshot).
96	  const back = eid || snapshot?.magicPresent ? clearActive : undefined;
97	
98	  // ---- gating states ----
99	  if (!eid) {
100	    // New, not-yet-saved visitor event (no eid, but the DOM saw the magic
101	    // address) → preview the detected guests + prompt to save.
102	    if (snapshot?.magicPresent) {
103	      return (
104	        <Shell onBack={back}>
105	          <NoticeState
106	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
107	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Add your guests and save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
108	            guests={snapshot.guestEmails}
109	          />
110	        </Shell>
111	      );
112	    }
113	    return (
114	      <Shell>
115	        <EmptyState
116	          auth={auth.data}
117	          events={visitorEvents.data}
118	          loading={visitorEvents.isLoading}
119	          onPick={(ev) => {
120	            chrome.storage.session.set({ [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: ev.eid });
121	            // Idea 4: also open that event in the same calendar tab.
122	            void rpc({ type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT', eventId: ev.eventId });
123	          }}
124	        />
125	      </Shell>
126	    );
127	  }
128	  if (isAuthError(resolve.error) || isAuthError(draft.error)) {
129	    return <Shell onBack={back}><SignInGate /></Shell>;
130	  }
131	  if (resolve.isLoading) return <Shell onBack={back}><LoadingState label="Reading event…" /></Shell>;
132	  if (resolve.isError) {
133	    const msg = (resolve.error as Error).message;
134	    if (msg === 'NOT_SAVED') {
135	      return (
136	        <Shell onBack={back}>
137	          <NoticeState
138	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
139	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
140	            onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
141	            guests={snapshot?.guestEmails}
142	          />
143	        </Shell>
144	      );
145	    }
146	    return (
147	      <Shell onBack={back}>
148	        <ErrorState message={msg} onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()} />
149	      </Shell>
150	    );
151	  }
152	  // Event resolved but no iCalUID → the draft query can't run (would sit on a
153	  // skeleton forever). Surface it instead of hanging.
154	  if (event && !event.iCalUid) {
155	    return (
156	      <Shell onBack={back}>
157	        <NoticeState
158	          title="Can’t register from this event"
159	          body="This event has no calendar UID yet — usually because it isn’t fully saved. Save it in Google Calendar and try again."
160	          onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
161	        />
162	      </Shell>
163	    );
164	  }
165	  if (draft.isError) {
166	    return (
167	      <Shell onBack={back}>
168	        <ErrorState message={(draft.error as Error).message} onRetry={() => draft.refetch()} />
169	      </Shell>
170	    );
171	  }
172	  if (!draft.data) {
173	    return <Shell onBack={back}><RosterSkeleton title={event?.title} /></Shell>;
174	  }
175	
176	  const data = draft.data;
177	  const included = data.roster.filter((g) => g.include);
178	  const sentCount = data.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent').length;
179	  const locValue = location ?? data.location ?? '';
180	
181	  const templates = data.emailTemplates;
182	  // One template for the whole event (engine stores per-guest; we set all guests
183	  // to the chosen key).
184	  const currentTemplate =
185	    data.roster.find((g) => g.emailTemplateKey)?.emailTemplateKey ??
186	    templates.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.key ??
187	    templates[0]?.key;
188	
189	  const patchGuest = (email: string, edit: Record<string, unknown>) =>
190	    patch.mutate({ guests: [{ email, ...edit }] });
191	
192	  const setEventTemplate = (key: string) =>
193	    patch.mutate({ guests: data.roster.map((g) => ({ email: g.email, emailTemplateKey: key })) });
194	
195	  // Review & send: render the real email for a representative included guest,
196	  // then Send/Cancel from the preview.
197	  const reviewEmail = included[0]?.email;
198	  const openReview = () => {
199	    if (!reviewEmail) return;
200	    preview.mutate(reviewEmail, {
201	      onSuccess: (d) => setPreviewData({ preview: d, recipient: reviewEmail }),
202	    });
203	  };
204	
205	  const canSend = (included.length > 0 || data.materialized) && !send.isPending;
206	  const reviewLabel = data.materialized ? 'Review & update passes' : 'Review & send passes';
207	
208	  return (
209	    <Shell onBack={back}>
210	      <div className="enter" style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
211	        <div
212	          className="type-label-sm text-muted"
213	          style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}
214	        >
215	          <Eye size={13} strokeWidth={2} />
216	          Following the event open in Calendar
217	        </div>
218	        {/* Event summary */}
219	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)' }}>
220	          <div className="type-title-lg">{event?.title || 'Untitled event'}</div>
221	          {event?.start && (
222	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
223	              <Clock size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
224	              {formatWhen(event.start, event.end)}
225	            </div>
226	          )}
227	          {event?.rooms && event.rooms.length > 0 && (
228	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
229	              <DoorOpen size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
230	              <span className="row__ellipsis">{event.rooms.join(', ')}</span>
231	            </div>
232	          )}
233	          {event?.description && (
234	            <div
235	              className="type-label-sm text-muted"
236	              style={{
237	                whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
238	                maxHeight: 72,
239	                overflow: 'hidden',
240	                borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
241	                paddingTop: 'var(--space-sm)',
242	              }}
243	            >
244	              {event.description}
245	            </div>
246	          )}
247	          <TextField
248	            label="Where visitors check in"
249	            value={locValue}
250	            placeholder="e.g. Front desk, 3rd floor"
251	            onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)}
252	            onBlur={() =>
253	              locValue !== (data.location ?? '') && patch.mutate({ location: locValue })
254	            }
255	          />
256	          {templates.length >= 2 && (
257	            <SelectField
258	              label="Email template (all guests)"
259	              value={currentTemplate}
260	              onChange={(e) => setEventTemplate(e.target.value)}
261	            >
262	              {templates.map((t) => (
263	                <option key={t.key} value={t.key}>
264	                  {t.name}
265	                  {t.isDefault ? ' (default)' : ''}
266	                </option>
267	              ))}
268	            </SelectField>
269	          )}
270	        </Card>
271	
272	        {/* Roster */}
273	        <section>
274	          <div
275	            className="type-label-sm text-muted"
276	            style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}
277	          >
278	            Guests · {included.length} getting a pass
279	          </div>
280	          {data.roster.length === 0 ? (
281	            <Card className="type-body text-muted">
282	              No guests on this event yet. Add attendees in Calendar, then reopen.
283	            </Card>
284	          ) : (
285	            <Card style={{ paddingTop: 0, paddingBottom: 0 }}>
286	              {data.roster.map((g) => (
287	                <RosterRow
288	                  key={g.email}
289	                  guest={g}
290	                  onChange={(edit) => patchGuest(g.email, edit)}
291	                />
292	              ))}
293	            </Card>
294	          )}
295	        </section>
296	
297	        {/* Send result */}
298	        {send.isSuccess && <SendSummary result={send.data} />}
299	        {send.isError && (
300	          <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert">
301	            <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
302	            <span className="type-body">{(send.error as Error).message}</span>
303	          </div>
304	        )}
305	      </div>
306	
307	      {/* Sticky action bar */}
308	      <footer
309	        style={{
310	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
311	          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
312	          background: 'var(--color-surface-lowest)',
313	        }}
314	      >
315	        <Button
316	          block
317	          loading={preview.isPending}
318	          disabled={!canSend}
319	          icon={<Eye size={18} strokeWidth={2} />}
320	          onClick={openReview}
321	        >
322	          {preview.isPending
323	            ? 'Loading preview…'
324	            : `${reviewLabel}${included.length ? ` (${included.length})` : ''}`}
325	        </Button>
326	        {preview.isError && (
327	          <div className="type-label-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--color-error)', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
328	            {(preview.error as Error).message}
329	          </div>
330	        )}
331	        {sentCount > 0 && !send.isPending && (
332	          <div className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
333	            {sentCount} pass{sentCount > 1 ? 'es' : ''} already issued
334	          </div>
335	        )}
336	      </footer>
337	
338	      {previewData && (
339	        <PreviewSheet
340	          preview={previewData.preview}
341	          recipient={previewData.recipient}
342	          totalCount={included.length}
343	          sending={send.isPending}
344	          onSend={() =>
345	            send.mutate(undefined, { onSuccess: () => setPreviewData(null) })
346	          }
347	          onClose={() => setPreviewData(null)}
348	        />
349	      )}
350	    </Shell>
351	  );
352	}
353	
354	// ───────────────────────── sub-components ─────────────────────────
355	
356	function Shell({ children, onBack }: { children: React.ReactNode; onBack?: () => void }) {
357	  return (
358	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', minWidth: 320 }}>
359	      <header
360	        style={{
361	          display: 'flex',
362	          alignItems: 'center',
363	          gap: 'var(--space-sm)',
364	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
365	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
366	          flex: '0 0 auto',
367	        }}
368	      >
369	        {onBack ? (
370	          <IconButton label="Back to events" onClick={onBack}>
371	            <ArrowLeft size={20} strokeWidth={2} />
372	          </IconButton>
373	        ) : (
374	          <Logo size={26} />
375	        )}
376	        <span className="type-title">Auxilio Visitor</span>
377	        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
378	        <AccountMenu />
379	      </header>
380	      <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
381	        {children}
382	      </div>
383	    </div>
384	  );
385	}
386	
387	function LoadingState({ label }: { label: string }) {
388	  return (
389	    <div
390	      style={{
391	        flex: 1,
392	        display: 'flex',
393	        flexDirection: 'column',
394	        alignItems: 'center',
395	        justifyContent: 'center',
396	        gap: 'var(--space-md)',
397	        color: 'var(--color-on-surface-variant)',
398	      }}
399	    >
400	      <span className="spinner" style={{ width: 22, height: 22 }} />
401	      <span className="type-body">{label}</span>
402	    </div>
403	  );
404	}
405	
406	function RosterSkeleton({ title }: { title?: string }) {
407	  return (
408	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
409	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 8 }}>
410	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title || 'Loading event…'}</span>
411	        <Skeleton width="60%" />
412	      </Card>
413	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
414	        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (

416	            <Skeleton width={44} height={24} radius={999} />
417	            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
418	              <Skeleton width="50%" />
419	              <Skeleton width="70%" height={12} />
420	            </div>
421	          </div>
422	        ))}
423	      </Card>
424	    </div>
425	  );
426	}
427	
428	function NoticeState({
429	  title,
430	  body,
431	  onRetry,
432	  guests,
433	}: {
434	  title: string;
435	  body: string;
436	  onRetry?: () => void;
437	  guests?: string[];
438	}) {
439	  return (
440	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
441	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)', padding: 'var(--space-xl)' }}>
442	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title}</span>
443	        <span className="type-body text-muted">{body}</span>
444	        {onRetry && (
445	          <Button
446	            variant="tonal"
447	            icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
448	            onClick={onRetry}
449	            style={{ justifySelf: 'start', marginTop: 'var(--space-xs)' }}
450	          >
451	            Check again
452	          </Button>
453	        )}
454	      </Card>
455	      {guests && guests.length > 0 && (
456	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-xs)' }}>
457	          <span className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
458	            Guests detected on this event
459	          </span>
460	          {guests.map((g) => (
461	            <span key={g} className="type-body row__ellipsis">{g}</span>
462	          ))}
463	        </Card>
464	      )}
465	    </div>
466	  );
467	}
468	
469	function ErrorState({ message, onRetry }: { message: string; onRetry: () => void }) {
470	  return (
471	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
472	      <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
473	        <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
474	        <span className="type-body">{message}</span>
475	      </div>
476	      <Button variant="tonal" icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />} onClick={onRetry}>
477	        Try again
478	      </Button>
479	    </div>
480	  );
481	}
482	
483	function SendSummary({
484	  result,
485	}: {
486	  result: import('@/lib/types').SendResponse;
487	}) {
488	  return (
489	    <div
490	      className="banner"
491	      role="status"
492	      aria-live="polite"
493	      style={{ background: 'var(--color-success-container)', color: 'var(--color-on-success-container)' }}
494	    >
495	      <CheckCircle2 size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
496	      <div>
497	        <div className="type-label">
498	          {result.created.length > 0
499	            ? `${result.created.length} pass${result.created.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''} sent`
500	            : 'Passes updated'}
501	          {result.cancelled.length > 0 && ` · ${result.cancelled.length} cancelled`}
502	        </div>
503	        {result.failed.length > 0 && (
504	          <ul className="type-label-sm" style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', paddingLeft: 16 }}>
505	            {result.failed.map((f) => (
506	              <li key={f.visitorEmail}>
507	                {f.visitorEmail}: {f.reason}
508	              </li>
509	            ))}
510	          </ul>
511	        )}
512	      </div>
513	    </div>
514	  );
515	}
516	
517	function formatWhen(start: string, end?: string): string {
518	  const allDay = !start.includes('T');
519	  const s = new Date(start);
520	  const date = s.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
521	  if (allDay) return `${date} · All day`;
522	  const t = (d: Date) => d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
523	  return end ? `${date} · ${t(s)}–${t(new Date(end))}` : `${date} · ${t(s)}`;
524	}
525	
```

1	/** MD3 switch. Controlled; instant visual feedback (optimistic at call site). */
2	export function Switch({
3	  checked,
4	  onChange,
5	  disabled,
6	  label,
7	}: {
8	  checked: boolean;
9	  onChange: (next: boolean) => void;
10	  disabled?: boolean;
11	  label?: string;
12	}) {
13	  return (
14	    <button
15	      type="button"
16	      role="switch"
17	      aria-checked={checked}
18	      aria-label={label}
19	      className="switch"
20	      disabled={disabled}
21	      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}
22	    >
23	      <span className="switch__thumb" />
24	    </button>
25	  );
26	}
27

1	import type { InputHTMLAttributes, ReactNode, SelectHTMLAttributes } from 'react';
2	
3	interface TextFieldProps extends InputHTMLAttributes<HTMLInputElement> {
4	  label?: string;
5	}
6	
7	/** Slim filled text input. */
8	export function TextField({ label, id, className, ...rest }: TextFieldProps) {
9	  return (
10	    <label className="field" htmlFor={id}>
11	      {label && <span className="field__label">{label}</span>}
12	      <input
13	        id={id}
14	        className={['field__input', className ?? ''].join(' ')}
15	        {...rest}
16	      />
17	    </label>
18	  );
19	}
20	
21	interface SelectFieldProps extends SelectHTMLAttributes<HTMLSelectElement> {
22	  label?: string;
23	  children: ReactNode;
24	}
25	
26	/** Native select styled to match the field. Used for ≥2 template choices. */
27	export function SelectField({ label, id, children, className, ...rest }: SelectFieldProps) {

29	    <label className="field" htmlFor={id}>
30	      {label && <span className="field__label">{label}</span>}
31	      <select
32	        id={id}
33	        className={['field__input', className ?? ''].join(' ')}
34	        {...rest}
35	      >
36	        {children}
37	      </select>
38	    </label>

1	/* Design system for the side panel document. CSS custom properties mirror
2	   design/tokens.ts. Component classes below are intentionally small wrappers
3	   over Material 3 values — no UI framework. */
4	
5	:root {
6	  /* color roles */
7	  --color-primary: #92288e;
8	  --color-on-primary: #ffffff;
9	  --color-primary-container: #f8d9f5;
10	  --color-on-primary-container: #310031;
11	
12	  --color-error: #ba1a1a;
13	  --color-on-error: #ffffff;
14	  --color-error-container: #ffdad6;
15	  --color-on-error-container: #410002;
16	
17	  --color-success: #3f6212;
18	  --color-success-container: #dcf2bc;
19	  --color-on-success-container: #142000;
20	
21	  --color-background: #fbf7fa;
22	  --color-on-background: #1e1a1d;
23	  --color-surface: #fbf7fa;
24	  --color-on-surface: #1e1a1d;
25	
26	  --color-surface-lowest: #ffffff;
27	  --color-surface-low: #f5eff3;
28	  --color-surface-container: #efe8ee;
29	  --color-surface-high: #e9e2e8;
30	  --color-surface-highest: #e3dde2;
31	
32	  --color-on-surface-variant: #4d444b;
33	  --color-outline: #7c747a;
34	  --color-outline-variant: #cdc4cb;
35	  --color-scrim: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.45);
36	
37	  /* state layer opacities (MD3) */
38	  --state-hover: 0.08;
39	  --state-focus: 0.12;
40	  --state-pressed: 0.12;
41	
42	  /* spacing */
43	  --space-xxs: 2px;
44	  --space-xs: 4px;
45	  --space-sm: 8px;
46	  --space-md: 12px;
47	  --space-lg: 16px;
48	  --space-xl: 24px;
49	  --space-xxl: 32px;
50	
51	  /* shape */
52	  --radius-xs: 4px;
53	  --radius-sm: 8px;
54	  --radius-md: 12px;
55	  --radius-lg: 16px;
56	  --radius-xl: 24px;
57	  --radius-pill: 999px;
58	
59	  /* motion */
60	  --motion-fast: 120ms;
61	  --motion-base: 200ms;
62	  --motion-slow: 320ms;
63	  --motion-easing: cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1);
64	  --motion-emphasized: cubic-bezier(0.3, 0, 0, 1);
65	
66	  /* elevation */
67	  --elev-1: 0 1px 2px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08);
68	  --elev-2: 0 2px 6px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
69	  --elev-3: 0 4px 12px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12);
70	}
71	
72	* {
73	  box-sizing: border-box;
74	}
75	
76	html,
77	body,
78	#root {
79	  height: 100%;
80	}
81	
82	body {
83	  margin: 0;
84	  background: var(--color-background);
85	  color: var(--color-on-background);
86	  font-family: 'Google Sans', 'Roboto', system-ui, -apple-system, sans-serif;
87	  font-size: 14px;
88	  line-height: 20px;
89	  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
90	}
91	
92	/* typography helpers */
93	.type-headline { font-size: 24px; line-height: 32px; font-weight: 700; }
94	.type-title-lg { font-size: 22px; line-height: 28px; font-weight: 600; }
95	.type-title { font-size: 16px; line-height: 24px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.15px; }
96	.type-body-lg { font-size: 15px; line-height: 22px; }
97	.type-body { font-size: 14px; line-height: 20px; letter-spacing: 0.25px; }
98	.type-label { font-size: 14px; line-height: 20px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.1px; }
99	.type-label-sm { font-size: 12px; line-height: 16px; font-weight: 600; letter-spacing: 0.5px; }
100	.text-muted { color: var(--color-on-surface-variant); }
101	
102	/* scrollbar — slim, unobtrusive */
103	::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 8px; height: 8px; }
104	::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
105	  background: var(--color-outline-variant);
106	  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
107	}
108	::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }
109	
110	/* ---------- Button ---------- */
111	.btn {
112	  display: inline-flex;
113	  align-items: center;
114	  justify-content: center;
115	  gap: var(--space-sm);
116	  border: none;
117	  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
118	  padding: 10px 20px;
119	  font: inherit;
120	  font-weight: 600;
121	  letter-spacing: 0.1px;
122	  cursor: pointer;
123	  position: relative;
124	  overflow: hidden;
125	  transition: box-shadow var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing),
126	    background-color var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing),
127	    opacity var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing);
128	  user-select: none;
129	}
130	.btn:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: default; pointer-events: none; }
131	.btn::after {
132	  content: '';
133	  position: absolute;
134	  inset: 0;
135	  background: currentColor;
136	  opacity: 0;
137	  transition: opacity var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing);
138	}
139	.btn:hover::after { opacity: var(--state-hover); }
140	.btn:active::after { opacity: var(--state-pressed); }
141	
142	.btn--filled { background: var(--color-primary); color: var(--color-on-primary); }
143	.btn--filled:hover { box-shadow: var(--elev-1); }
144	.btn--tonal { background: var(--color-primary-container); color: var(--color-on-primary-container); }
145	.btn--text { background: transparent; color: var(--color-primary); padding: 10px 12px; }
146	.btn--danger { background: var(--color-error); color: var(--color-on-error); }
147	.btn--block { display: flex; width: 100%; }
148	
149	/* ---------- Card ---------- */
150	.card {
151	  background: var(--color-surface-lowest);
152	  border: 1px solid var(--color-outline-variant);
153	  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
154	  padding: var(--space-lg);
155	}
156	
157	/* ---------- Skeleton ---------- */
158	.skeleton {
159	  background: linear-gradient(
160	    90deg,
161	    var(--color-surface-high) 25%,
162	    var(--color-surface-highest) 37%,
163	    var(--color-surface-high) 63%
164	  );
165	  background-size: 400% 100%;
166	  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
167	  animation: skeleton-shimmer 1.4s ease infinite;
168	}
169	@keyframes skeleton-shimmer {
170	  0% { background-position: 100% 50%; }
171	  100% { background-position: 0 50%; }
172	}
173	
174	/* ---------- Spinner ---------- */
175	.spinner {
176	  display: inline-block;
177	  width: 16px;
178	  height: 16px;
179	  border: 2px solid currentColor;
180	  border-right-color: transparent;
181	  border-radius: 50%;
182	  animation: spinner-rotate 0.7s linear infinite;
183	}
184	@keyframes spinner-rotate { to { transform: rotate(360deg); } }
185	
186	/* ---------- Switch (MD3) ---------- */
187	.switch {
188	  position: relative;
189	  display: inline-flex;
190	  flex: 0 0 auto;
191	  width: 44px;
192	  height: 24px;
193	  border-radius: var(--radius-pill);
194	  border: 2px solid var(--color-outline);
195	  background: var(--color-surface-highest);
196	  cursor: pointer;
197	  transition: background-color var(--motion-base) var(--motion-easing),
198	    border-color var(--motion-base) var(--motion-easing);
199	  padding: 0;
200	}
201	.switch__thumb {
202	  position: absolute;
203	  top: 50%;
204	  left: 4px;
205	  width: 12px;
206	  height: 12px;
207	  border-radius: 50%;
208	  background: var(--color-outline);
209	  transform: translate(0, -50%);
210	  transition: transform var(--motion-base) var(--motion-emphasized),
211	    width var(--motion-base) var(--motion-emphasized),
212	    height var(--motion-base) var(--motion-emphasized),
213	    background-color var(--motion-base) var(--motion-easing);
214	}
215	.switch[aria-checked='true'] {
216	  background: var(--color-primary);
217	  border-color: var(--color-primary);
218	}
219	.switch[aria-checked='true'] .switch__thumb {
220	  width: 16px;
221	  height: 16px;
222	  background: var(--color-on-primary);
223	  transform: translate(18px, -50%);
224	}
225	.switch:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: default; }
226	
227	/* ---------- Text field (filled, slim) ---------- */
228	.field { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-xxs); }
229	.field__label {
230	  font-size: 12px;
231	  font-weight: 600;
232	  letter-spacing: 0.4px;
233	  color: var(--color-on-surface-variant);
234	}
235	.field__input {
236	  font: inherit;
237	  color: var(--color-on-surface);
238	  background: var(--color-surface-low);
239	  border: 1px solid var(--color-outline-variant);
240	  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
241	  padding: 8px 10px;
242	  outline: none;
243	  transition: border-color var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing),
244	    box-shadow var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing);
245	}
246	.field__input:focus {
247	  border-color: var(--color-primary);
248	  box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--color-primary);
249	}
250	.field__input::placeholder { color: var(--color-outline); }
251	select.field__input { cursor: pointer; appearance: none; }
252	
253	/* ---------- Icon button ---------- */
254	.icon-btn {
255	  display: inline-flex;
256	  align-items: center;
257	  justify-content: center;
258	  width: 36px;
259	  height: 36px;
260	  border: none;
261	  border-radius: 50%;
262	  background: transparent;
263	  color: var(--color-on-surface-variant);
264	  cursor: pointer;
265	  position: relative;
266	  overflow: hidden;
267	  transition: color var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing);
268	}
269	.icon-btn::after {
270	  content: '';
271	  position: absolute;
272	  inset: 0;
273	  background: currentColor;
274	  opacity: 0;
275	  transition: opacity var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-easing);
276	}
277	.icon-btn:hover::after { opacity: var(--state-hover); }
278	.icon-btn:active::after { opacity: var(--state-pressed); }
279	.icon-btn:disabled { opacity: 0.4; pointer-events: none; }
280	
281	/* ---------- Chip ---------- */
282	.chip {
283	  display: inline-flex;
284	  align-items: center;
285	  gap: 4px;
286	  height: 22px;
287	  padding: 0 8px;
288	  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
289	  font-size: 11px;
290	  font-weight: 600;
291	  letter-spacing: 0.3px;
292	}
293	.chip--neutral { background: var(--color-surface-high); color: var(--color-on-surface-variant); }
294	.chip--success { background: var(--color-success-container); color: var(--color-on-success-container); }
295	.chip--error { background: var(--color-error-container); color: var(--color-on-error-container); }
296	
297	/* ---------- List row ---------- */
298	.row {
299	  display: flex;
300	  align-items: center;
301	  gap: var(--space-md);
302	  padding: var(--space-md) 0;
303	}
304	.row + .row { border-top: 1px solid var(--color-outline-variant); }
305	.row--dim { opacity: 0.55; }
306	.row__grow { flex: 1 1 auto; min-width: 0; }
307	.row__ellipsis {
308	  overflow: hidden;
309	  text-overflow: ellipsis;
310	  white-space: nowrap;
311	}
312	
313	/* ---------- Banner (Phase 2 / inline messages) ---------- */
314	.banner {
315	  display: flex;
316	  align-items: flex-start;
317	  gap: var(--space-sm);
318	  padding: var(--space-md);
319	  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
320	}
321	.banner--error { background: var(--color-error-container); color: var(--color-on-error-container); }
322	.banner--info { background: var(--color-primary-container); color: var(--color-on-primary-container); }
323	
324	/* keyboard focus ring (focus-visible only — no ring on mouse) */
325	:where(button, a, input, select, [tabindex]):focus-visible {
326	  outline: 2px solid var(--color-primary);
327	  outline-offset: 2px;
328	}
329	.field__input:focus-visible {
330	  outline: none;
331	  border-color: var(--color-primary);
332	  box-shadow: 0 0 0 2px var(--color-primary);
333	}
334	.switch:focus-visible { outline-offset: 3px; }
335	
336	/* fade/slide-in for panel content */
337	.enter {
338	  animation: enter var(--motion-base) var(--motion-easing) both;
339	}
340	@keyframes enter {
341	  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(6px); }
342	  to { opacity: 1; transform: none; }
343	}
344	
345	/* honor user preference */
346	@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
347	  *,
348	  *::before,
349	  *::after {
350	    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
351	    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
352	    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
353	  }
354	}

1	/**
2	 * Design tokens — web port of field-app/src/design/tokens.ts.
3	 * Material 3 *values* (not its code), tuned for our brand ("Steady Purple",
4	 * primary #92288E). The CSS custom properties in global.css mirror these;
5	 * keep the two in sync. Prefer the CSS vars in components; this TS object is
6	 * for places that need values in JS (e.g. injected shadow-DOM UI).
7	 */
8	
9	export const palette = {
10	  primary: '#92288E',
11	  onPrimary: '#FFFFFF',
12	  primaryContainer: '#F8D9F5',
13	  onPrimaryContainer: '#310031',
14	
15	  secondary: '#6E5868',
16	  onSecondary: '#FFFFFF',
17	  secondaryContainer: '#F8DAEF',
18	  onSecondaryContainer: '#271624',
19	
20	  error: '#BA1A1A',
21	  onError: '#FFFFFF',
22	  errorContainer: '#FFDAD6',
23	  onErrorContainer: '#410002',
24	
25	  warn: '#7A5900',
26	  onWarn: '#FFFFFF',
27	  warnContainer: '#FFDF9E',
28	  onWarnContainer: '#261A00',
29	
30	  success: '#3F6212',
31	  onSuccess: '#FFFFFF',
32	  successContainer: '#DCF2BC',

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/design/components/Switch.tsx",
  "content": "import { Check } from 'lucide-react';\n\n/** MD3 switch. Controlled; instant visual feedback (optimistic at call site).\n *  The thumb carries a check glyph when on — MD3's signature \"selected\" cue,\n *  so the state reads at a glance instead of relying on colour alone. */\nexport function Switch({\n  checked,\n  onChange,\n  disabled,\n  label,\n}: {\n  checked: boolean;\n  onChange: (next: boolean) => void;\n  disabled?: boolean;\n  label?: string;\n}) {\n  return (\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      role=\"switch\"\n      aria-checked={checked}\n      aria-label={label}\n      className=\"switch\"\n      disabled={disabled}\n      onClick={() => onChange(!checked)}\n    >\n      <span className=\"switch__thumb\">\n        <Check className=\"switch__check\" size={11} strokeWidth={3.5} />\n      </span>\n    </button>\n  );\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/features/visit/RosterRow.tsx",
  "content": "import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';\nimport { Phone, User } from 'lucide-react';\nimport { Chip, Switch, TextField } from '@/design/components';\nimport type { DraftGuest } from '@/lib/types';\n\ntype GuestEdit = {\n  include?: boolean;\n  name?: string;\n  phone?: string;\n};\n\n/** Two-letter monogram from a name (\"Jane Doe\" → \"JD\") or an email local-part\n *  (\"jane.doe@x\" → \"JD\"), so each guest has a stable, recognisable avatar. */\nfunction monogram(seed: string): string {\n  const parts = seed.trim().split(/[\\s._-]+/).filter(Boolean);\n  if (parts.length === 0) return '?';\n  if (parts.length === 1) return parts[0].slice(0, 2).toUpperCase();\n  return (parts[0][0] + parts[1][0]).toUpperCase();\n}\n\nexport function RosterRow({\n  guest,\n  onChange,\n}: {\n  guest: DraftGuest;\n  onChange: (edit: GuestEdit) => void;\n}) {\n  const [name, setName] = useState(guest.name);\n  const [phone, setPhone] = useState(guest.phone ?? '');\n\n  // Keep local inputs in sync when the server reconciles the row.\n  useEffect(() => setName(guest.name), [guest.name]);\n  useEffect(() => setPhone(guest.phone ?? ''), [guest.phone]);\n\n  const sent = guest.status === 'sent';\n  const cancelled = guest.status === 'cancelled';\n  const display = guest.name || guest.email;\n  const editable = guest.include && !sent;\n\n  return (\n    <div className={`guest${guest.include ? '' : ' guest--dim'}`}>\n      <div className=\"guest__head\">\n        <span\n          className={\n            'guest__avatar' +\n            (sent ? ' guest__avatar--sent' : guest.include ? '' : ' guest__avatar--dim')\n          }\n          aria-hidden\n        >\n          {monogram(guest.name || guest.email.split('@')[0])}\n        </span>\n\n        <div className=\"guest__id\">\n          <div className=\"guest__name\">\n            <span className=\"type-label row__ellipsis\">{display}</span>\n            {guest.internal && !sent && <Chip>Internal</Chip>}\n            {sent && <Chip tone=\"success\">Pass sent</Chip>}\n            {cancelled && <Chip tone=\"error\">Cancelled</Chip>}\n          </div>\n          <div className=\"type-label-sm text-muted row__ellipsis\">{guest.email}</div>\n        </div>\n\n        <Switch\n          checked={guest.include}\n          disabled={sent}\n          onChange={(v) => onChange({ include: v })}\n          label={`${guest.include ? 'Don’t invite' : 'Invite'} ${guest.email}`}\n        />\n      </div>\n\n      {editable ? (\n        <div className=\"guest__form\">\n          <TextField\n            label=\"Visitor name\"\n            value={name}\n            leadingIcon={<User size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}\n            onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)}\n            onBlur={() => name !== guest.name && onChange({ name })}\n            placeholder=\"Full name\"\n          />\n          <TextField\n            label=\"Phone (for WhatsApp pass)\"\n            value={phone}\n            inputMode=\"tel\"\n            leadingIcon={<Phone size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}\n            onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}\n            onBlur={() => phone !== (guest.phone ?? '') && onChange({ phone })}\n            placeholder=\"+91…\"\n          />\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        !guest.include &&\n        !cancelled && (\n          <div className=\"guest__hint type-label-sm\">Won’t receive a pass — toggle on to invite</div>\n        )\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Both type-check and build pass. Here's what I changed and why, framed against our MD3-tokens stance.

## What was wrong
The per-guest "card" (`RosterRow`) was really a flat list row: a bare switch on the left, a name string, then plain stacked inputs that appeared below. With `.row { align-items: center }`, the switch floated to the vertical middle of the whole row once the inputs expanded — so the control drifted away from the thing it controlled. There was no identity anchor, no contained grouping for the edit fields, and toggling "off" silently did nothing visible, so the switch's meaning wasn't obvious.

## What I did (all on our own tokens — no framework added)

**`RosterRow.tsx` — proper MD3 list item.**
- **Avatar monogram** (e.g. "JD" from name, or the email local-part) anchors each guest visually and recolors by state: primary-container normally, success when a pass is sent, muted when excluded.
- Identity (name + chips + email) sits as the headline/supporting text; the **switch is now trailing and vertically centered against the identity block** — it stays put whether the row is collapsed or expanded.
- The edit fields live in a **recessed `surface-low` container indented under the name**, so they read as "this guest's details," with a subtle expand animation (respecting `prefers-reduced-motion` via the existing global rule).
- **Honest feedback for "off":** an excluded guest now shows *"Won't receive a pass — toggle on to invite"* instead of just dimming silently, so the toggle teaches its own meaning.

**`Switch.tsx` — MD3's signature checkmark.** The thumb now carries a check glyph that fades in when on. State reads at a glance instead of relying on color/position alone.

**`TextField.tsx` — leading-icon support.** New optional `leadingIcon` prop (Lucide only). The name field gets a `User` icon, the phone field a `Phone` icon; the icon picks up the primary color on focus.

**`global.css`** — added `.guest*` styles, `.field__icon`/`--with-icon`, and `.switch__check`; kept `.row*` intact since `EmptyState` and others still use those.

A couple of judgment calls worth surfacing: I kept the switch size at 44×24 (this is a desktop, mouse-driven Chrome surface, not the low-end Android baseline), and I added the leading-icon capability to the shared `TextField` rather than one-off styling, since it's reusable across the extension. If you'd rather the edit form not be indented (some prefer full-width on a narrow side panel), that's a one-line change.

I haven't committed anything, per repo rules. Want me to load the unpacked build and screenshot it, or adjust the visual direction first?

> AGENT

Confirmed — this is not a Google URL change. The real `/eventedit/` eid encodes the event id **plus the calendar id**, and for your primary calendar that segment is your email (`junaid@appointy.com`). Google hasn't changed anything.

The bug is on our side, in the **"Manage" / nudge path only**:
- The content-script button passes the page's real `data-eventid` (already contains the email) → works.
- But the sync/nudge events are encoded in `lib/calendar-sync.ts:54` as `encodeEid(ev.id, 'primary')` — the literal string `"primary"`. The `NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT` handler uses that eid verbatim for the URL, so you land on `…primary`, which Google's web editor can't resolve to the event.

The comment at `background.ts:256` even states the intent ("primary calendar id = the user's email") — it's just only applied on the `eventId`-only branch, not when an `eid` is already present.

The right fix is at the navigation boundary: re-encode the eid with the real email when its calendar segment is `primary`. I'm deliberately **not** changing the stored eid in `calendar-sync.ts`, because `primary` is the correct, valid calendar id for the Calendar **API** resolution path (`resolveCanonicalEvent`) — only the *web editor URL* needs the email.

Let me check the imports and apply it:

> AGENT

Exact match. To summarize:

## Answer: No, Google didn't change the URL

Both eids decode to the **same event id** (`786oqbqr7623gebqn84efa059g`). The only difference is the calendar-id segment that's encoded alongside it:

| | calendar-id segment |
|---|---|
| Google's real editor URL | `junaid@appointy.com` (your primary calendar's id) |
| What "Manage" was opening | `primary` (literal string) |

Google's `/r/eventedit/<eid>` expects the eid to be `base64("<eventId> <realCalendarId>")`, and for the primary calendar the real id is your email — not the word `"primary"`. The Calendar **API** happens to accept `"primary"` as an alias, which is why resolution/data still worked, but the **web editor URL** can't resolve it, so "Manage" landed on a broken/blank edit page.

## Fix
Our sync/nudge events were encoded as `encodeEid(ev.id, 'primary')` (`lib/calendar-sync.ts:54`), and `NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT` used that eid verbatim. I added a step in the navigation handler (`background.ts`) that, just before building the URL, rewrites any eid whose calendar segment is `primary` (or empty) to use the signed-in user's email. I verified the rebuilt eid is byte-for-byte identical to Google's.

I intentionally left `calendar-sync.ts` and the resolution path on `primary` — that's the correct, valid alias for the API, and only the web-editor URL needs the email form. The content-script button path was already correct (it carries the page's real `data-eventid`).

Type-check passes. Worth a quick manual confirm: trigger the nudge → "Manage" and check it opens the real event editor now. Want me to load the unpacked build to verify?

> AGENT

The fix is in the rebuilt bundle (`eventedit/${n}` template and the `calendarId` decode logic are present in `.output/chrome-mv3/background.js`). The decoded URL you got — `3agj174jc6rr10j86s5lcd51on primary` — still has the literal `primary`, which means the **previous build was still loaded**. Code edits don't go live until the extension is reloaded.

To pick up the fix:

1. **`chrome://extensions`** → find Auxilio Visitor → click the **reload** (↻) icon. (If you run `npm run dev`, stop/restart it; WXT writes to `.output/chrome-mv3`, but the loaded copy still needs a reload.)
2. **Reload your Google Calendar tab** — the content script that owns the "Manage" button is injected per-tab and won't update until the page reloads.
3. Press **Manage** again and check the URL.

It should now end with the base64 of `<eventId> junaid@appointy.com` instead of `…primary`. I verified the re-encoder reproduces Google's exact eid byte-for-byte for your earlier example.

If after a clean reload it *still* shows `primary`, then `email` isn't reaching the handler — that would be a different, diagnosable problem (stale token cache without the `email` claim). In that case, sign out and back in once (so a fresh id_token repopulates `email`), and tell me — I'd then add a hard fallback that fetches the primary calendar's real id from the Calendar API rather than trusting the literal `primary`. But based on the code path, a reload should be all it takes.

1	/**
2	 * Calendar sync (background only). The durable, official "what changed" engine:
3	 * runs on a chrome.alarm, lists changes via a sync token, and maintains the set
4	 * of upcoming visitor (magic-address) events in storage.local. Drives:
5	 *  - post-save refresh of the open event (changedIds),
6	 *  - the visitor-event notification + toolbar badge (newMarked / count),
7	 *  - the side panel's "open from list" picker (listMarked).
8	 * No DOM. Reflects SAVED state only (pre-save edits are add-on-only — see
9	 * Planning-docs/2026-06-19_calendar_addon_vs_extension_capabilities.md).
10	 */
11	import { encodeEid, isMarked, listEvents } from './calendar';
12	import type { VisitorEventSummary } from './types';
13	
14	const SYNC_TOKEN_KEY = 'auxilio.syncToken';
15	const MARKED_KEY = 'auxilio.markedEvents';
16	const PAST_GRACE_MS = 60 * 60_000; // keep events until 1h after they end
17	
18	type MarkedMap = Record<string, VisitorEventSummary>;
19	
20	async function readMarked(): Promise<MarkedMap> {
21	  const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(MARKED_KEY);
22	  return (r[MARKED_KEY] as MarkedMap) ?? {};
23	}
24	
25	export interface SyncResult {
26	  changedIds: Set<string>;
27	  newMarked: VisitorEventSummary[];
28	  markedCount: number;
29	}
30	
31	/** Pull changes since the last sync; update the marked set; report deltas. */
32	export async function runSync(accessToken: string): Promise<SyncResult> {
33	  const store = await chrome.storage.local.get([SYNC_TOKEN_KEY, MARKED_KEY]);
34	  let syncToken = store[SYNC_TOKEN_KEY] as string | undefined;
35	  let marked = (store[MARKED_KEY] as MarkedMap) ?? {};
36	
37	  let resp = await listEvents(accessToken, { syncToken });
38	  if (resp.expired) {
39	    // Token stale → full resync from scratch.
40	    syncToken = undefined;
41	    marked = {};
42	    resp = await listEvents(accessToken, {});
43	  }
44	
45	  const changedIds = new Set<string>();
46	  const newMarked: VisitorEventSummary[] = [];
47	
48	  for (const ev of resp.items) {
49	    if (!ev.id) continue;
50	    changedIds.add(ev.id);
51	    const cancelled = ev.status === 'cancelled';
52	    if (isMarked(ev) && !cancelled) {
53	      const summary: VisitorEventSummary = {
54	        eid: encodeEid(ev.id, 'primary'),
55	        eventId: ev.id,
56	        iCalUid: ev.iCalUID ?? '',
57	        title: ev.summary || '(no title)',
58	        start: ev.start?.dateTime ?? ev.start?.date,
59	      };
60	      if (!marked[ev.id]) newMarked.push(summary);
61	      marked[ev.id] = summary;
62	    } else {
63	      delete marked[ev.id]; // unmarked or cancelled
64	    }
65	  }
66	
67	  // Prune events that are well past.
68	  const cutoff = Date.now() - PAST_GRACE_MS;
69	  for (const id of Object.keys(marked)) {
70	    const s = marked[id].start;
71	    if (s && Date.parse(s) < cutoff) delete marked[id];
72	  }
73	
74	  await chrome.storage.local.set({
75	    [SYNC_TOKEN_KEY]: resp.nextSyncToken ?? syncToken,
76	    [MARKED_KEY]: marked,
77	  });
78	
79	  return { changedIds, newMarked, markedCount: Object.keys(marked).length };
80	}
81	
82	/** Upcoming visitor events, soonest first — for the panel's picker. */
83	export async function listMarked(): Promise<VisitorEventSummary[]> {
84	  const marked = await readMarked();
85	  return Object.values(marked).sort((a, b) =>
86	    (a.start ?? '').localeCompare(b.start ?? ''),
87	  );
88	}
89	
90	/** Is this event id currently a known visitor event? */
91	export async function isEventMarked(eventId: string): Promise<boolean> {
92	  const marked = await readMarked();
93	  return !!marked[eventId];
94	}
95

110	    function readSurface(): { el: HTMLElement; eid: string } | null {
111	      const fromUrl = urlEid();
112	      const dialog = document.querySelector('[role="dialog"]') as HTMLElement | null;
113	      if (fromUrl) {

125	    function snapshotOf(el: HTMLElement, eid: string): DomEventSnapshot {
126	      const text = el.innerText || '';
127	      const lower = text.toLowerCase();
128	      const magicPresent = !!MAGIC_ADDRESS && lower.includes(MAGIC_ADDRESS);
129	      const guestEmails = extractEmails(text).filter(
130	        (e) => e.toLowerCase() !== MAGIC_ADDRESS,
131	      );
132	      // Best-effort title (cosmetic — the API sync replaces it). The editor's
133	      // title is a text input; fall back to undefined.
134	      const titleInput = el.querySelector(
135	        'input[aria-label*="title" i], input[placeholder*="title" i]',
136	      ) as HTMLInputElement | null;
137	      const title = titleInput?.value?.trim() || undefined;
138	      return { eid, magicPresent, guestEmails, title };
139	    }

141	    function render() {
142	      const surface = readSurface();
143	      lastSnapshot = surface ? snapshotOf(surface.el, surface.eid) : null;
144	
145	      // Instant nudge: while viewing a visitor event WITH a real eid, remember
146	      // it; when the user then leaves that surface (closed popover / saved &
147	      // left editor), show the nudge for it IMMEDIATELY — no API round-trip.
148	      if (lastSnapshot?.magicPresent && lastSnapshot.eid) {
149	        pendingMagic = {
150	          eid: lastSnapshot.eid,
151	          eventId: decodeEventId(lastSnapshot.eid),
152	          title: lastSnapshot.title ?? '',
153	        };
154	      } else if (!surface && pendingMagic) {
155	        nudge.addOptimistic({
156	          eid: pendingMagic.eid,
157	          eventId: pendingMagic.eventId,
158	          iCalUid: '',
159	          title: pendingMagic.title,
160	          start: undefined,
161	        });
162	        syncBurst(); // confirm/refresh from the API right after
163	        pendingMagic = null;
164	      }

> AGENT

I now have the full picture. Here's the root cause and the fix.

## Root cause

The "Visitor event needs passes" banner has two sources:
- **Authoritative sync** (`calendar-sync.ts`) — correctly drops `cancelled`/deleted events (`:51,:63`).
- **Optimistic, DOM-derived nudge** (`calendar.content.ts:148-164`) — fires the instant a magic-address event surface *disappears*, on the assumption "saved & left."

**Deleting an event closes its surface exactly like saving-and-leaving does.** So the optimistic path fires for the just-deleted event. Worse, `setTargets` only clears an optimistic entry when a matching *real* target arrives from sync (`:600`) — a deleted event never will, so the stale nudge lingers and "Manage" opens the panel for a ghost event.

## Fix

Make the authoritative sync purge the optimistic guess: thread the set of `cancelled` event ids from sync → background → content, and drop them from the optimistic map. Plus a TTL safety-net so any unconfirmed optimistic guess self-expires instead of lingering forever.

Let me implement.

> TOOL

> AGENT

Both pass. Here's what I changed and why.

## What was happening
Deleting a magic-address event fired the **optimistic, DOM-derived nudge** in the content script. That path (`calendar.content.ts:148-164`) shows the banner the instant an event's surface disappears, assuming "saved & left" — but **deletion closes the surface identically**, so it nudged for the ghost event. And since optimistic entries were only ever cleared when a matching *real* synced target arrived (which never happens for a deleted event), the stale nudge persisted and "Manage" opened the panel for it.

## The fix (sync is the source of truth)
1. **`calendar-sync.ts`** — `runSync` now collects `cancelledIds`: events seen this cycle that are `cancelled` (deleted) or dropped out of the marked set.
2. **`background.ts`** — `broadcastNudge` forwards those ids to calendar tabs alongside the marked targets.
3. **`calendar.content.ts`** — `setTargets(targets, cancelled)` **purges** any optimistic guess the sync resolved away, so a deleted event can't keep nudging or reopen the panel.
4. **TTL safety-net** — every optimistic guess now self-expires after 12s unless the sync promotes it to a real target. This catches the deletion case *and* any other unconfirmed guess (already-handled, magic address removed) instead of letting it linger forever.

I deliberately did **not** try to detect deletion from the DOM (snackbar text / grid chips) — that's i18n-fragile and risks suppressing legitimate nudges. Routing the truth through the sync is robust and self-correcting.

## One honest caveat
There's still a brief window: the banner can flash for a just-deleted event until the burst sync confirms the cancellation. The burst fires at 0/3/8s, but it only clears once **Google's events.list API** reflects the deletion (their eventual-consistency lag), then the purge removes it; the 12s TTL is the hard backstop. Eliminating the flash entirely would require DOM deletion-sniffing, which isn't worth the fragility. If that flash proves annoying in practice, the next lever is a short confirm-delay before the optimistic fires — tell me and I'll add it.

Needs a rebuild + extension reload + Calendar tab reload to take effect (same as before). Want me to run `/code-review` on this diff?

1	/**
2	 * Content script on Google Calendar — the DOM layer of the hybrid model.
3	 *
4	 * It reads the open event surface (detail popover / expanded edit) to:
5	 *  - GATE the "Manage Visitors" button on the magic address being a guest,
6	 *  - capture the `data-eventid` (base64url "<eventId> <calendarId>"), and
7	 *  - take a best-effort SNAPSHOT (guest emails) so the panel can paint instantly
8	 *    and cover the unsaved (no-API) case.
9	 * The API layer (background) remains the authoritative source. We read only the
10	 * semantic `data-eventid`, the `[role="dialog"]` ARIA signal, and the surface's
11	 * visible text — no layout/CSS-class coupling, no injection into Google's markup.
12	 */
13	import { MAGIC_ADDRESS } from '@/lib/config';
14	import {
15	  ACTIVE_EID_KEY,
16	  ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY,
17	  NUDGE_TARGETS,
18	  PANEL_STATE,
19	} from '@/lib/messaging';
20	import type { DomEventSnapshot, VisitorEventSummary } from '@/lib/types';
21	
22	export default defineContentScript({
23	  matches: ['https://calendar.google.com/*'],
24	  main() {
25	    // The id of the most recently clicked event chip — the event whose popover
26	    // is opening. Reset when the surface closes so a later unrelated dialog
27	    // can't resurrect a stale button.
28	    let clickedEid: string | null = null;
29	    let lastSnapshot: DomEventSnapshot | null = null;
30	    let panelOpen = false;
31	    let followedEid: string | null = null; // last event auto-pushed to the panel
32	    let pendingMagic: { eid: string; eventId: string; title: string } | null = null;
33	    let everSawEventId = false;
34	
35	    // Button UI: a native injected button when a clean anchor exists, else a
36	    // floating fallback (Idea 2). The magic-address gate is dropped — the button
37	    // shows on any open event surface; clicking it IS the intent.
38	    const button = createButtonUI(() => {
39	      if (!lastSnapshot) return;
40	      openFor(lastSnapshot); // opens the panel + sets the active event
41	      // Take the user to the full EDIT screen for this event — unless they're
42	      // already on it (avoid a reload that would drop unsaved edits). The
43	      // snapshot eid (from data-eventid) is already the right /eventedit eid.
44	      if (lastSnapshot.eid && !urlEid()) {
45	        chrome.runtime
46	          .sendMessage({ type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT', eid: lastSnapshot.eid })
47	          .catch(() => {});
48	      }
49	    });
50

'').trim().toLowerCase() === wanted) return el;\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\nconst isVisible = (el: HTMLElement) => el.offsetParent !== null || el.getClientRects().length > 0;\n\n/** The Description editor on the event edit page. ARIA/placeholder text only —\n *  never CSS classes (Google's are obfuscated and churn). */\nfunction findEditDescription(): HTMLElement | null {\n  const els = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(\n    '[aria-label*=\"description\" i], [placeholder*=\"description\" i]',\n  );\n  for (const el of els) if (isVisible(el)) return el;\n  return null;\n}\n\n/** Climb from a field to its \"section row\" — the child of the form's sections\n *  list — so we can insert a sibling *above the whole block*, not inside it. The\n *  sections list is the first ancestor with several element children. */\nfunction sectionAncestor(el: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {\n  let node: HTMLElement = el;\n  while (node.parentElement && node.parentElement !== document.body) {\n    if (node.parentElement.childElementCount >= 3) return node;\n    node = node.parentElement;\n  }\n  return node;\n}\n\n/** The organiser line inside the detail popover, or null. Matches the visible\n *  \"Organiser/Organizer\" label, then returns its section row so we can drop the\n *  button right below it. */\nfunction findOrganiserRow(dialog: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {\n  const els = dialog.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*');\n  for (const el of els) {\n    const t = (el.textContent ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();\n    if ((t === 'organiser' || t === 'organizer') && isVisible(el)) {\n      return sectionAncestor(el);\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}"
}

> AGENT

Both pass. Here's what I changed.

## Behavior now
`findAnchor()` (in `calendar.content.ts`) places the **Manage Visitors** button *inside* the surface, with a graceful fallback chain:

1. **Event edit page** (`/eventedit`) → injected **just above the Description section** in the details tab. Fallback: before the native **Save** button (the old spot). 
2. **Detail popover / modal** → injected **right below the organiser line**, at the bottom. Fallback: appended to the very bottom of the dialog.
3. **Floating button** → only if neither surface offers an anchor (now a true last resort, not the default for popovers).

The injected button gets `block` styling (its own line, `margin:12px 0 4px`) for the new in-surface spots, and stays inline (`margin-right:8px`) only for the before-Save fallback.

## How it stays robust
- **Text/ARIA only, never CSS classes** — per the content-script contract. Description is found via `[aria-label*="description" i]`/placeholder; the organiser via the visible "Organiser/Organizer" label text; sections via a structural climb (`sectionAncestor`) to the form's section row.
- **Self-healing** — the existing `MutationObserver` re-runs `render()`, and `ensureInjected` re-injects if Google re-renders and drops our button. I also reordered `ensureInjected` to skip the anchor scan once the button is already placed (no per-tick popover scanning).
- Layered fallbacks mean a DOM change at Google's end degrades to a sensible spot rather than vanishing.

## One honest caveat
The exact placement depends on Google Calendar's current DOM, which I can't inspect from here. The Description `aria-label`, the literal "Organiser/Organizer" label, and the "section = parent with ≥3 children" heuristic are best-effort and English/ARIA-based. If the live result is off by a row, it's a one-selector tune — the structure is isolated in `findEditDescription` / `findOrganiserRow` / `sectionAncestor`.

Needs the usual rebuild + extension reload + Calendar tab reload. Want me to launch it and verify the placement visually (via the `verify`/`run` flow), or will you check it on your side first?

308	/**
309	 * Button UI manager (Idea 2): renders a NATIVE-styled button injected at a clean
310	 * anchor (next to Save on the /eventedit page) when one is found, and otherwise
311	 * falls back to a floating button. `update(show)` decides per render.
312	 */
313	function createButtonUI(onClick: () => void) {
314	  const fab = mountFloating(onClick);
315	  let injected: HTMLButtonElement | null = null;
316	
317	  type Anchor = { insert: (el: HTMLElement) => void; block: boolean };
318	
319	  /** A clean injection anchor, or null. Text/ARIA-based, never CSS classes, so
320	   *  we stay decoupled from Google's obfuscated markup. Prefers the user-facing
321	   *  spots: above Description on the edit page, below the organiser in the
322	   *  detail popover. Falls back to the floating button only when neither fits. */
323	  function findAnchor(): Anchor | null {
324	    // Full-screen edit page → just above the Description section.
325	    if (/\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname)) {
326	      const desc = findEditDescription();
327	      if (desc) {
328	        const section = sectionAncestor(desc);
329	        const parent = section.parentElement;
330	        if (parent) return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, section), block: true };
331	      }
332	      // Fallback within the edit page: right before the native "Save" button.
333	      const save = findButtonByText('save');
334	      const parent = save?.parentElement;
335	      if (save && parent) return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, save), block: false };
336	      return null;
337	    }
338	    // Detail popover / modal → at the bottom, just below the organiser line.
339	    const dialog = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role="dialog"]');
340	    if (dialog) {
341	      const org = findOrganiserRow(dialog);
342	      const parent = org?.parentElement;
343	      if (org && parent) {
344	        return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, org.nextSibling), block: true };
345	      }
346	      // No organiser line found → append at the very bottom of the dialog.
347	      return { insert: (el) => dialog.appendChild(el), block: true };
348	    }
349	    return null;
350	  }
351	
352	  function ensureInjected(): boolean {
353	    if (injected && injected.isConnected) return true; // still placed — skip the scan
354	    const anchor = findAnchor();
355	    if (!anchor) return false;
356	    removeInjected();
357	    injected = buildInjectedButton(onClick, anchor.block);
358	    anchor.insert(injected);
359	    return true;
360	  }
361	
362	  function removeInjected() {
363	    injected?.remove();
364	    injected = null;
365	  }
366	
367	  return {
368	    update(show: boolean) {
369	      if (!show) {
370	        removeInjected();
371	        fab.setVisible(false);
372	        return;
373	      }
374	      const placed = ensureInjected();
375	      fab.setVisible(!placed); // floating only when we couldn't inject natively
376	    },
377	  };
378	}

384	  for (const el of els) {

  "new_string": "function createButtonUI(onClick: () => void) {\n  const fab = mountFloating(onClick);\n  let injected: HTMLElement | null = null;\n\n  // A clean injection anchor: where to drop the button + how far to indent it so\n  // it lines up with the section's content column (past Google's icon gutter).\n  // `inline` = sit next to a native button instead of as its own row.\n  type Anchor = { insert: (el: HTMLElement) => void; inset: number; inline?: boolean };\n\n  /** Text/ARIA-based only — never CSS classes — so we stay decoupled from\n   *  Google's obfuscated markup. Places the button where it reads as part of the\n   *  form: between Description and the next section on the edit page, and as the\n   *  last row of the detail popover's content list. Floating only as last resort. */\n  function findAnchor(): Anchor | null {\n    // Full-screen edit page → a row of its own *between* Description and the\n    // following section (e.g. Visibility).\n    if (/\\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname)) {\n      const desc = findEditDescription();\n      if (desc) {\n        const section = sectionAncestor(desc);\n        const parent = section.parentElement;\n        if (parent) {\n          const inset = contentInset(section, desc);\n          return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, section.nextSibling), inset };\n        }\n      }\n      // Fallback within the edit page: inline, right before the native \"Save\".\n      const save = findButtonByText('save');\n      const parent = save?.parentElement;\n      if (save && parent) return { insert: (el) => parent.insertBefore(el, save), inset: 0, inline: true };\n      return null;\n    }\n    // Detail popover / modal → the LAST row of the content list, aligned under\n    // the organiser line above it.\n    const dialog = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[role=\"dialog\"]');\n    if (dialog) {\n      const org = findOrganiserLabel(dialog);\n      if (org) {\n        const row = sectionAncestor(org);\n        const list = row.parentElement;\n        if (list) {\n          const inset = contentInset(row, org);\n          return { insert: (el) => list.appendChild(el), inset };\n        }\n      }\n      // No organiser line found → append at the very bottom of the dialog.\n      return { insert: (el) => dialog.appendChild(el), inset: 0 };\n    }\n    return null;\n  }\n\n  function ensureInjected(): boolean {\n    if (injected && injected.isConnected) return true; // still placed — skip the scan\n    const anchor = findAnchor();\n    if (!anchor) return false;\n    removeInjected();\n    ensureInjectedStyles();\n    injected = anchor.inline\n      ? buildInlineButton(onClick)\n      : buildInjectedRow(onClick, anchor.inset);\n    anchor.insert(injected);\n    return true;\n  }"
}

'').trim().toLowerCase();\n    if ((t === 'organiser' || t === 'organizer') && isVisible(el)) {\n      return sectionAncestor(el);\n    }\n  }\n  return null;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Climb from a field to its \"section row\" — the child of the form's sections\n *  list — so we can insert a sibling *between sections*, not inside one. The\n *  sections list is the first ancestor with several element children. */\nfunction sectionAncestor(el: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {\n  let node: HTMLElement = el;\n  while (node.parentElement && node.parentElement !== document.body) {\n    if (node.parentElement.childElementCount >= 3) return node;\n    node = node.parentElement;\n  }\n  return node;\n}\n\n/** Horizontal gap between a section row's left edge and where its content\n *  begins (Google indents content past a leading icon gutter). Lets our row\n *  line up with the native fields instead of starting at the icon. */\nfunction contentInset(row: HTMLElement, content: HTMLElement): number {\n  const gap = content.getBoundingClientRect().left - row.getBoundingClientRect().left;\n  return Number.isFinite(gap) && gap > 0 && gap < 120 ? Math.round(gap) : 0;\n}\n\n/** The organiser label inside the detail popover, or null. Matches the visible\n *  \"Organiser/Organizer\" text so we can sit our row just below that line. */\nfunction findOrganiserLabel(dialog: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {\n  const els = dialog.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*');\n  for (const el of els) {\n    const t = (el.textContent ?? '').trim().toLowerCase();\n    if ((t === 'organiser' || t === 'organizer') && isVisible(el)) return el;\n  }\n  return null;\n}"
}

'margin:12px 0 4px' : 'margin-right:8px',\n    'cursor:pointer',\n    'flex:0 0 auto',\n    'white-space:nowrap',\n  ].join(';');\n  btn.innerHTML = `${USERS_SVG}<span>${LABEL}</span>`;\n  shieldInteractions(btn);\n  wireActivate(btn, onClick);\n  return btn;\n}",
  "new_string": "const STYLE_ID = 'auxilio-manage-visitors-style';\n\n/** One-time stylesheet for the injected (light-DOM) button. Styled to match a\n *  native Material outlined Calendar button — same shape, size, typography and\n *  hover state-layer — so it reads as part of Google's UI, just brand-tinted. */\nfunction ensureInjectedStyles() {\n  if (document.getElementById(STYLE_ID)) return;\n  const s = document.createElement('style');\n  s.id = STYLE_ID;\n  s.textContent = `\n    .auxilio-mv-row{box-sizing:border-box;display:flex;align-items:center;width:100%;padding:8px 0;}\n    .auxilio-mv-btn{\n      box-sizing:border-box;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;\n      font-family:'Google Sans','Roboto',Arial,sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:500;\n      line-height:20px;letter-spacing:.25px;color:#92288e;background:transparent;\n      border:1px solid #747775;border-radius:999px;padding:8px 18px 8px 14px;\n      min-height:40px;cursor:pointer;white-space:nowrap;\n      transition:background-color 120ms ease,border-color 120ms ease;\n    }\n    .auxilio-mv-btn:hover{background:rgba(146,40,142,.08);}\n    .auxilio-mv-btn:active{background:rgba(146,40,142,.12);}\n    .auxilio-mv-btn:focus-visible{outline:2px solid #92288e;outline-offset:2px;}\n    .auxilio-mv-btn[disabled]{opacity:.6;cursor:default;}\n    .auxilio-mv-btn svg{width:18px;height:18px;flex:0 0 auto;}\n  `;\n  document.documentElement.appendChild(s);\n}\n\n/** The native-styled button itself (light DOM, classes from ensureInjectedStyles). */\nfunction makeNativeButton(onClick: () => void): HTMLButtonElement {\n  const btn = document.createElement('button');\n  btn.type = 'button';\n  btn.className = 'auxilio-mv-btn';\n  btn.innerHTML = `${USERS_SVG}<span>${LABEL}</span>`;\n  shieldInteractions(btn);\n  wireActivate(btn, onClick);\n  return btn;\n}\n\n/** A full-width row carrying the button, indented to align with the section\n *  content column. Flows like a native section, so it can't overlap siblings. */\nfunction buildInjectedRow(onClick: () => void, inset: number): HTMLElement {\n  const row = document.createElement('div');\n  row.id = INJECT_ID;\n  row.className = 'auxilio-mv-row';\n  if (inset) row.style.paddingLeft = `${inset}px`;\n  row.appendChild(makeNativeButton(onClick));\n  // Guard only the row's empty padding — clicks there must not dismiss Google's\n  // surface; the button manages its own interactions (shieldInteractions).\n  shieldPadding(row);\n  return row;\n}\n\n/** Inline button for the before-Save fallback (sits next to a native action). */\nfunction buildInlineButton(onClick: () => void): HTMLButtonElement {\n  const btn = makeNativeButton(onClick);\n  btn.id = INJECT_ID;\n  btn.style.marginRight = '8px';\n  return btn;\n}\n\n/** Stop interactions on the row's own padding (but not the button) from reaching\n *  Google — so an accidental tap beside the button can't close the dialog. */\nfunction shieldPadding(row: HTMLElement) {\n  const guard = (e: Event) => {\n    if (e.target === row) {\n      e.preventDefault();\n      e.stopPropagation();\n    }\n  };\n  for (const type of ['pointerdown', 'mousedown', 'touchstart', 'click'] as const) {\n    row.addEventListener(type, guard, true);\n  }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Build and type-check pass. Here's the reworked injection.

## What changed

**No more bare-button overlap.** Instead of dropping a `<button>` into Google's section markup, I now inject a **full-width row** (`.auxilio-mv-row`, `display:flex; width:100%`) that flows as its own block — it can't overlap siblings. The button sits inside that row, indented via `contentInset()` to line up with the section's content column (measured as the gap past Google's leading icon gutter, clamped 0–120px).

**Placement matches your spec:**
- **Edit page** → inserted **between Description and the next section** (Visibility) via `section.nextSibling`, as its own row.
- **Modal/popover** → appended as the **last row of the content list** (the organiser line's row container), aligned under the organiser text above it.
- **Floating** → now a genuine last resort, only when no in-surface anchor exists.

**Native look.** The button is restyled as a Material **outlined** Calendar button via a one-time stylesheet: `Google Sans` 14/500, pill radius, `min-height:40px`, 1px `#747775` border, a proper hover/active **state-layer** and `:focus-visible` ring. It's brand-tinted (purple text/icon `#92288e`) rather than a solid purple fill, so it reads as part of Google's UI rather than an overlay.

**Interaction safety.** The button keeps its own `shieldInteractions`; the new row adds `shieldPadding`, which blocks taps on the row's *empty padding* (not the button) from bubbling to Google and dismissing the dialog. The `MutationObserver` still re-injects if Google re-renders.

## Honest caveat
I'm still inferring Google's DOM (the `aria-label*="description"` match, the literal "Organiser/Organizer" label, the "section = parent with ≥3 children" climb, and the inset measurement). These are isolated in `findEditDescription` / `findOrganiserLabel` / `sectionAncestor` / `contentInset`, so tuning is a one-spot change — but I can't confirm pixel placement without seeing it live.

Given we've now iterated on DOM specifics a few times, I'd suggest I **launch the extension and inspect the real Calendar DOM** so I can lock the selectors/alignment exactly instead of inferring. Want me to do that via the verify/run flow? It needs loading the unpacked build in Chrome and a Google login on your side.

> AGENT

Got it — two real placement bugs from my heuristics:

1. **Edit screen**: `sectionAncestor`'s "parent with ≥3 children" matched a *horizontal* flex row (icon | text | value), so my row landed as an inline flex item to the right of the availability text instead of as its own stacked row.
2. **Modal**: anchoring on the organiser's row container isn't the bottom of all content.

The fix is to find the actual **vertical** content column (by geometry, not child-count) and append our row at the bottom of it. Let me rewrite `findAnchor` and the helpers.

id: toolu_015ewMXE2qercbDT2tkuy1KS
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/entrypoints/calendar.content.ts",
  "old_string": "/** Climb from a field to its \"section row\" — the child of the form's sections\n *  list — so we can insert a sibling *between sections*, not inside one. The\n *  sections list is the first ancestor with several element children. */\nfunction sectionAncestor(el: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {\n  let node: HTMLElement = el;\n  while (node.parentElement && node.parentElement !== document.body) {\n    if (node.parentElement.childElementCount >= 3) return node;\n    node = node.parentElement;\n  }\n  return node;\n}",
  "new_string": "/** Does this element stack its visible children vertically (a column of rows),\n *  rather than laying them out side by side? Geometry, not CSS classes. */\nfunction stacksVertically(el: HTMLElement): boolean {\n  const kids = [...el.children].filter((c): c is HTMLElement => c instanceof HTMLElement && isVisible(c));\n  if (kids.length < 2) return false;\n  for (let i = 1; i < kids.length; i++) {\n    const a = kids[i - 1].getBoundingClientRect();\n    const b = kids[i].getBoundingClientRect();\n    if (b.top >= a.bottom - 2) return true; // the next child starts below the previous\n  }\n  return false;\n}\n\n/** Climb from a field to its full-width \"section row\" — the topmost ancestor\n *  that fills its parent's width AND whose parent stacks its children into a\n *  column. That parent is the vertical sections list; the returned node is the\n *  row we sit a sibling next to (so our button is its own row, never inline). */\nfunction fullRowAncestor(el: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {\n  let node: HTMLElement = el;\n  let best: HTMLElement = el;\n  while (node.parentElement && node.parentElement !== document.body) {\n    const parent = node.parentElement;\n    const nodeW = node.getBoundingClientRect().width;\n    const parentW = parent.getBoundingClientRect().width;\n    if (parentW > 0 && nodeW >= parentW * 0.8 && stacksVertically(parent)) best = node;\n    node = parent;\n  }\n  return best;\n}\n\n/** The biggest vertically-stacking container inside `root` — the content column\n *  whose last child is the visual bottom of the surface. */\nfunction mainVerticalList(root: HTMLElement): HTMLElement | null {\n  let best: HTMLElement | null = null;\n  let bestArea = 0;\n  for (const el of root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('*')) {\n    if (!isVisible(el) || !stacksVertically(el)) continue;\n    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();\n    const area = r.width * r.height;\n    if (area > bestArea) {\n      bestArea = area;\n      best = el;\n    }\n  }\n  return best;\n}"
}
